# FMZ survey summary (branch `survey`)

Generated by `survey_tools/summarize.py` from `screening.csv`, `review_decisions.csv`,
`duplicates.csv` and `ports/`. Corpus: 5,806 FMZ strategy files on `master` (commit 7853bb2),
each linking to its FMZ page (`https://www.fmz.com/strategy/<id>`). Sources read 2026-09-29.
Nothing was backtested, run or ranked; FMZ performance claims were not used (criterion 7).

Method, contract and interpretation rules: `SURVEY_README.md`. Status and next steps: `PROGRESS.md`.

## Where every file stands now

| Outcome | Files |
|---|---:|
| PORTED | 2159 |
| PORT_CANDIDATE | 1275 |
| REJECTED | 994 |
| HELD_NEEDS_VOLUME | 567 |
| REJECTED_ON_READING | 306 |
| FLAGGED_CRYPTO_ONLY | 253 |
| DUPLICATE | 251 |
| DUPLICATE_ON_READING | 1 |
| **Total** | **5806** |

`PORT_CANDIDATE` = passed the static screen and is waiting to be read and ported.
`REJECTED_ON_READING` = screen passed it, reading showed it fails (reasons below).

### Criterion 6 (near-duplicates)

269 groups; 596 files in groups; 327 collapsed onto their representative. Every group, with its representative and collapsed ids, is in `duplicates.csv`; 370 near-threshold pairs to check by hand are in `possible_duplicates.csv`.

## Ports (criteria 4 and 5)

Each port folder holds `module.py`, `original_source.md` (verbatim), `original_sizing.txt`
(verbatim sizing and money-management code, labelled with line numbers) and `PORT_NOTES.md`.

| 187874 | 标定值或均线交叉策略.md | 2 | Entries and exits fire on hard-coded BTC price levels (REF(C,1) < 6725 buys, > 10000 sells, 'Gann levels'). Without them only an MA(10/30) cross remains, which would be a different strategy. |
| 191622 | 割庄神器.md | 1 | Order-level ladder on perpetual swaps: opens on a daily-range test of > 20 price units, then keeps resting limit orders k = 11 price units above/below the fill, averaging in and martingale-style profit targets; fills inside the bar at set prices (also hard-coded price units, criterion 2). |
| 201007 | 抛硬币策略.md | 1 | Coin-flip strategy: entries and exits are drawn from Math.random(); no deterministic signal to port. (Its trailing take-profit and stop are attached to random entries.) |
| 205469 | bybit-swap永续加仓策略.md | 1 | One-direction accumulation ladder on a perpetual swap: buys (or sells) a fixed USD slice on every bar the MA filter holds, doubles it after two counter bars, scales out after three with-trend bars, caps total size; no exit other than a live-only bar-count stop. Position size is the strategy. |
| 255502 | 恒温器-震荡修正为-最高价-最低价-添加增减仓选项-添加额外张数功能-修正cmi数据刷新日期问一天一次变为1小时一次-ok-准备修正一些功能.md | 1 | Two concurrent sub-systems (CMI shock / trend) each holding its own hedged futures position with ATR-step scale-in or scale-out ladders, extra-lot counters and departure callbacks. | 266142 | 均仓策略.md | 1 | Pure 50/50 coin/cash rebalancing (buy or sell 1 % / 10 % slices when the coin share leaves 0.49-0.51); no entry or exit signal. |
| 299799 | ahr999定投策略.md | 2 | AHR999 dollar-cost averaging: the indicator is a Bitcoin-only model (price vs a log-price curve fitted to days since the 2009 genesis block); the code throws for any other pair. Also periodic accumulation, not entries/exits. |
| 361719 | Demark-Reversal-Points.md | 1 | Signals come from request.security(syminfo.tickerid, '18000', src)[1]: '18000' is not a valid Pine resolution (minutes? seconds?), so the higher timeframe the counts run on is undefined. Porting would mean choosing a bar size (rule 1 forbids); needs the project to define it. |
| 363557 | Pivot-Points-High-Low-Multi-Time-Frame.md | 1 | Pivots come from request.security(syminfo.tickerid, '240', get_phpl(), lookahead_on) without [1]: on historical bars the 4 h pivot is visible from the first 5 m bar of the 4 h bar that confirms it, i.e. it reads the future (SURVEY_README request.security rule). |
| 363572 | OCC-Strategy-R51.md | 1 | Both MA series are read through request.security(..., stratRes, lookahead_on) without [1] (alternate resolution on by default, 3x the chart period): on historical bars the higher-timeframe values are visible before that bar closes, i.e. they read the future. |
| 365389 | TradingView-Alerts-to-MT4-MT5-dynamic-variables-NON-REPAINTING.md | 1 | The exit is a two-step ladder: 50 % of the position at a 150-tick profit (qty_percent=50), the rest at 400 ticks or a pivot stop; a partial exit cannot be expressed as one net position's signals, and tick distances are instrument-specific (an MT4 alert template; criterion 2 too). |
| 368717 | Inverted-Hammer-Extended-Options.md | 1 | Long entries only (inverted hammer below EMA 10), with no exit, stop or reversal anywhere: after the first signal the position is held for the rest of the data, so there is no repeatable entry/exit rule to test (as #62163). |
| 368734 | Brick-count-Renko.md | 1 | The orders test bar counts as booleans: `if brick_red` (non-green bars among the last 40) is true unless 40 green bars in a row, so the script is long on practically every bar; the brick cross it labels never reaches the orders. No defined signal to test. |
| 369999 | Directional-Movement-Oscillator-DMI.md | 1 | The orders test a float and a plot handle as booleans: `if diosc` (DI+ - DI-, true whenever non-zero) -> long, `else if p2` (a plot id) -> short. Long on practically every bar; the short branch depends on how the runtime casts a plot handle. No defined signal to test. |
| 370728 | Smooth-HA-Candles-MTF-v1.md | 1 | The orders read a nested request: security(heikinashi(ticker), 'D', x) where x is itself security(ticker, 'D', open[1], lookahead_on). Which daily bar (and whether Heikin-Ashi or regular prices) reaches the orders depends on how the runtime resolves a nested request on a different ticker; the higher-timeframe values are undefined without choosing (as #361719). |
| 380446 | BTC-bot.md | 1 | The exit is a four-step take-profit ladder (25 % of the position at +3 %, +5 %, +7 %, the rest at +10 %, all with a 15 % stop, in ticks): partial exits cannot be expressed as one net position's signals (as #365389). |
| 380530 | Trade-Hour-V3.md | 1 | strategy.order (not entry) adds a unit on every bar whose hour equals an hour estimated from cumulative ROC x hour statistics since the first bar, with no exit or reversal: the position is a running sum of orders, so the size path is the strategy. |
| 392636 | MACD-Strategy.md | 1 | The exit is a partial take-profit ladder (10 % of the position at +2 %, 50 % at +5 %, the rest on a close below an ATR stop): partial exits cannot be expressed as one net position's signals (as #365389). pyramiding=2 also stacks entries. |
| 395966 | Exit-from-specific-entries.md | 1 | A Pine documentation example: two pyramided units opened on Monday / Tuesday and closed by entry id on Thursday / Friday; the outcome is a position-size ladder by weekday, not entries/exits of one net position. |
| 416875 | MartinGale-Strategy1.md | 1 | Martingale: every second it buys or sells a growing bet according to the last candle's colour, multiplying the size after wins and losses and stopping after four losses. The position size sequence is the strategy (tick loop as well). |
| 422794 | 布林带策略止盈和马丁格尔倍投.md | 1 | Martingale ladder: pyramiding=6 and strategy.order adds strategy.position_size * martinFactor to a losing position (lines 55-67); the position size depends on fills, not on a bar rule (as #395966, #416875). |
| 425798 | Trade01高低轨道线均线.md | 1 | The entry and exit tests read BKVOL <> 1 / BKVOL = 1 / SKVOL = 1 (exactly one lot held), so the rules change with the sizing formula LOTS; and the stop multiplier LIQKA is a plain (non-VARIABLE) MyLanguage name decremented each held bar (lines 102-108), whose persistence across bars is undefined. No defined signal to test. |
| 426261 | The-Open-Range-Strategy-with-Dynamic-Profit-Target.md | 1 | The opening range and the entry window come from session strings ('0930-1100', '0930-1000', '1000-1100') read through time() and security() at 1- and 30-minute resolutions on the 1h header chart: the exchange time zone of a crypto pair and what a lower-resolution time()/security() returns per chart bar are undefined, so the bars that may trade are not defined (as #361719). |
| 426302 | The-Bollinger-Band-Automated-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | 3Commas DCA bot: up to 6 safety orders add strategy.position_size * 1.55 below the entry (strategy.order), and the stop / target are fractions of the averaged position price; the position ladder is the strategy (as #422794). |
| 426334 | Uptrend-and-Oversold-Index-Swing-Trading-System-strategy.md | 1 | The whole signal is SCORE, which calls ta.ema(close, n) for n = 1..21 inside one for-loop call site: the EMA's recursive state (its [1] value) is shared across the 21 calls, so what each length reads is defined by the runtime, not the script. Also a trailing stop order from highs and buys blocked for the rest of the calendar day after any fill. |
| 426361 | Composite-Entry-Signal-RSI-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The only exit is strategy.exit('close', 'buy') with no profit / loss / stop / limit / trail argument (an error on TradingView, no exit level on any runtime), so a long entered on the MA crossing 40 has no defined exit (as #368717). |
| 426364 | KD-Dual-direction-Tracking-Strategy.md | 1 | KD inventory model: strategy.order adds or removes one default unit per bar until the position reaches a target share count (0.33 steps of 20 / -10 shares); the position size path is the strategy (as #380530). |
| 426455 | Enhanced-Fish-Net-Strategy.md | 1 | Four strategy.exit calls share the id 'Exit' with no from_entry: two set a 300-tick stop with a 150 / 50-tick trailing stop, two (when= buy / sell) set no exit level at all. | 426478 | 双Gann通道突破买卖策略Gann-Double-Channel-Breakout-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The signal is a crossover of security(tickerid, '375', close) and security(..., open) on the daily header chart: a 375-minute resolution below the chart's, whose value per daily bar (and 375-minute block alignment on a 24 h market) is undefined (as #426261). |
| 426556 | 周末区间震荡策略Weekend-Range-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | Weekend range bot on 2-day header bars: the reference is security(ticker, 'D', close[days_since_friday]), a daily (lower-than-chart) resolution whose value per 2-day bar is undefined (as #426261), and the weekday rules (Saturday / Sunday entries, Monday close) cannot be resolved on 2-day bars. | 426570 | 海龟空头突破加仓策略Turtle-Short-Breakout-Pyramiding-Strategy.md | 1 | Turtle short with pyramiding = 5: up to five 'P' adds every pyramidInput x N below the last add, with the stop and the win / loss bookkeeping driven by the averaged price of the stacked units (a pyramided ladder, as #395966). |

| 427066 | 日线收盘价对比策略Daily-Close-Price-Comparison-Strategy.md | 1 | Reads the future: Pine v2 security(syminfo.tickerid, 'D', close) without [1] behaves as lookahead_on (the v2-to-v3 converter adds lookahead_on to keep it), so on every intraday bar 'today' is the final close of the current day, which is not known until the day ends (SURVEY_README request.security rule, as #363557). | 427127 | 形态识别吊颈线交易策略Pattern-Recognition-Pinbar-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The stop is low[barsSinceEntry] x (1 - range / avg price), i.e. built from the ENTRY (fill) bar's own low and range, and it is placed only at that bar's close (active from the next bar). Porting it would change the exit. |

| 427306 | SuperTrend双均线交叉策略SuperTrend-Dual-Moving-Average-Crossover-Strategy.md | 1 | Both signal lines are request.security(syminfo.tickerid, '30', ...) on the daily header chart: a 30-minute resolution below the chart's, whose value per daily bar is undefined (as #426261 / #426478). |

| 427379 | 基于255-EMA-与-MACD-的反转交易策略255-EMA-and-MACD-Reversal-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The stop and target come from valuewhen(position_size changed, lowest(20) / highest(20), 0), i.e. the 20-bar swing measured on the FILL bar itself (its own low / high included), and are active from the next bar: a stop from the fill bar's data cannot be expressed (as #427127). |
| 427477 | 基于关键价格点的追踪策略Round-Number-Tracking-Strategy.md | 2 | The signal is the distance to a round-number price level built in ticks (roundLevel = close[1] / mintick rounded to 500 ticks, distance in 100 ticks, 10-bar lows / highs against that level): hard-coded price levels and tick distances specific to the instrument and its tick size. | 434936 | Y-Profit-Maximizer策略Y-Profit-Maximizer-Strategy.md | 1 | Partial exits: the long is scaled out with limit orders for 33.3 %, then 50 %, then the rest (qty_percent), driven by counters of the script's own take-profit levels (as #365389). |
| 435081 | 移动平均数RSI相关性加密货币趋势策略Moving-Average-RSI-Crypto-Correlation-Trend-Strategy.md | 3 | The signals are computed on request.security('BTC_USDT:swap', timeframe.period, close / open / high / low) ("correlation candles" on by default): a crypto-exchange perpetual contract used as the signal source for the chart symbol (as #430742). Stored in flagged/ (rule C7). |
| 435088 | 跨周期价值区域突破策略Cross-period-Value-Area-Breakout-Strategy.md | 1 | The momentum values are request.security(syminfo.tickerid, '180', rsi(...)) on the daily header chart: a 180-minute resolution below the chart's, whose value per daily bar is undefined (as #427518). |
| 435138 | 多时间轴均线系统交易策略Multi-Timeframe-Moving-Average-System-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The trend lines are request.security(syminfo.tickerid, '15' / '30' / '60' / '120', ...) on the 4-hour header chart: resolutions below the chart's, whose value per 4-hour bar is undefined (as #427518). |
| 435153 | 乖离矩阵趋势跟随策略Divergence-Matrix-Trend-Following-Strategy.md | 1 | Matrix ladder: the position is resized up and down around grid levels 2 % apart (increase / decrease / reset orders keyed on the entry price and the level count); the position is a sum of grid units (as #430596). |
| 435154 | 移动均线交叉交易策略EMA-Crossover-Trading-Strategy.md | 2 | The entries need close > entrybar, where entrybar is a var set to the first bar's close and never updated: a fixed price level set by where the chart's data starts. |
| 435256 | 指数修正分析过滤策略Modulo-Logic-With-EMA-Filter-Strategy.md | 2 | The entries need the close divided by 4 to leave no remainder (close % 4 == 0 by default): an exact test of the price against a multiple of a price-unit divisor, so the trigger is a fixed price level grid that means something different on every instrument and at every price scale; the stops are also built from valuewhen levels of the entry bar. |
| 435262 | MACD金叉突破配200日均线的趋势追踪策略MACD-Golden-Cross-Breakout-with-200-Day-Moving-Average-Trend-Following-Strategy.md | 2 | The stop level is set ABOVE the entry close (close x (1 + 0.5 %)) and kept in a var; the exits close the long whenever the close is under that level (true at once and on every later bar until the next signal) or at or over a target level, and strategy.exit is handed the price levels as loss / profit tick distances: the rules compare the close with price levels carried from the last signal and, as written, open and close on the same open or never hold, so no signal rule can be separated from the price-level bookkeeping. |
| 435275 | 基于逆转与相对强度的组合量化策略Quantitative-Strategy-Based-on-Reversal-and-Comparative-Relative-Strength.md | 1 | The relative-strength half compares the chart symbol with a second symbol read through security(b, timeframe.period, close) (the input default is the BTC swap itself, a ratio of 1.0) and tests the ratio's 10-bar average against price-ratio bands 0.9988 / 0.9960 / 0.9975: a two-instrument pair signal, outside the single-instrument interface. |
| 435278 | 未来麦克D路线预测策略MacD-Future-Path-Prediction-Strategy.md | 1 | The orders read the 4-hour MACD histogram through security(syminfo.tickerid, '240', hist, gaps_off, lookahead_on) with 'Use The Future?' true by default, so on the 5-minute chart each 4-hour bar's final histogram is seen from its first bar: it reads the future (SURVEY_README request.security rule); the other branch is the same logic without lookahead and is only a switch. |
| 435473 | 最低点反转策略Bottom-Reversal-Strategy.md | 1 | The entry opens 2 units and the exits are two separate 1-unit partial closes (strategy.close with qty = 1 on a cross under the ema of H4 and another on the ema of L3): the position is half-closed on the first exit signal and the rest on the second, a scale-out the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express; the ATR trailing level is only plotted. |
| 435486 | 印度市场整合突破交易策略Consolidation-Breakout-Strategy.md | 2 | Entries need a 09:35-14:30 session test (time(timeframe.period, session)) on the DAILY chart of the backtest header, where the bar's own time is not inside the session, so the entry condition is a clock-time filter that is undefined (probably never true) on the chart it was tested on; the exit is a stop re-sent each bar at close - 10 ATR (a close-relative level, not a ratchet) and the 14:57 end-of-day close never occurs on daily bars. |
| 435511 | 基于TRSI和SUPER趋势指标的量化交易策略Quantitative-Trading-Strategy-Based-on-TRSI-and-SUPER-Trend-Indicators.md | 1 | The exits are four partial exits of the position (strategy.exit with qty_percent 25 / 25 / 25 and the rest at profit levels 2 / 4 / 6 / 8 % of the average price, tick-converted) and the rule reads a weekly RSI of a held week-open close through time('W'): a scale-out in four parts that the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 435721 | 多重时间框架魔改加强的史托克指标与SMA组合趋势跟踪策略X48-DayLight-Hunter-Strategy-Optimization-and-Adaptation.md | 1 | The default Trend mode adds martingale orders (strategy.order Long2 / Short2 with qty = last size x 2 once the price has moved a point range against the entry), takes profit when strategy.openprofit reaches a currency amount (tp_pnl = 1 dollar, close_all) and sizes the percent stops by ticks: the exits and adds depend on the position size and its dollar profit, which the all-in signal interface cannot express. |
| 435725 | 基于开盘价通道趋势策略Channel-Trend-Strategy.md | 1 | The long and short each carry strategy.exit with qty_percent = 50 at a take-profit level of the channel line plus / minus 15 % (a scale-out of half the position) while the channel line itself is the highest / lowest of the monthly OPEN taken over 21 chart bars: the half-position exit cannot be expressed by the all-in / all-out signal interface. |
| 435766 | 基于MACD和RSI指标的交叉信号策略Trading-Strategy-Based-on-MACD-and-RSI-Crossover-Signals.md | 1 | Positions are built and cut by quantity: strategy.order buys 25 % of equity on each MACD-cross-after-oversold signal (pyramiding 10) and sells one third of the open position size (qty = posSize / 3) on each cross after overbought: scale-in and a stepped scale-out that the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 435773 | 坚实踏实的SMA均线持仓策略Solid-and-Steady-SMA-Position-Holding-Strategy.md | 1 | Two take-profit exits take 10 % and 50 % of the position at +1 % and +5 % (qty_percent), the rest following the ZLEMA slope exit: partial exits (a scale-out) that the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 435831 | SMA波动偏移交易策略SMA-Offset-Fluctuation-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The long's stop is an exit order armed only when the short condition appears during the trade (strategy.exit with stop = a ratcheted level computed from every bar's close, re-sent only on those bars, and enterShort needs strategy.openprofit > 0): an exit placed mid-trade at a level that depends on the position's open profit, outside the entry-fill bracket the stop interface supports. |
| 435839 | 超趋势跟踪止损策略Super-Trend-Tracking-Stop-Loss-Strategy.md | 1 | Each entry also sends strategy.exit with qty_percent = 10 at a profit target (a 10 % partial take profit; the 15 % and 20 % tiers are declared and unused) and the stop is a custom ratchet on bar closes: a partial exit that the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 435845 | 动量震荡跨界Bollinger带均线策略Momentum-Oscillation-Crossing-Bollinger-Bands-with-Moving-Average-Strategy.md | 1 | A 3Commas dollar-cost-averaging bot: strategy.order adds safety orders (volume-scaled quantities, up to a set count) on deviations below the average price while a base order is open, with take profit and stop on the average of the combined position: a ladder of adds whose size depends on the position, which the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 435850 | 动态MA均线交叉趋势追踪策略Dynamic-MA-Crossover-Trend-Following-Strategy.md | 1 | The moving averages are read through security(tickerid, '5D', sma(hlc3, 16), gaps_off, lookahead_on) and the matching EMA with lookahead_on and no [1]: on historical bars the 5-day values are seen from the first bar of the 5-day period, so the rule reads the future (SURVEY_README request.security rule). |
| 435964 | 基于布林带和RSI的趋势追踪策略Bollinger-Bands-and-RSI-Trend-Following-Strategy.md | 1 | The exit orders are re-sent every bar with levels computed from the CURRENT bar's extremes (sl = high + 50 ticks, tp = low - 100 ticks for a short; the mirror for a long): a moving bracket that follows each new bar rather than a level fixed at the entry fill, in tick units, which the stop interface (levels fixed at the entry fill or a declared trail) cannot express. |
| 436093 | 基于互动模式的K线交易策略-Interactive-Model-Based-Candlestick-Trading-Strategy.md | 2 | The entry needs the close over a fixed RESISTANCE price (200) and the exit a close under a fixed SUPPORT price (100): price levels typed in as inputs, which mean something different at every price scale and on every instrument. |
| 436096 | Ichimoku云量化短线策略Ichimoku-Cloud-Quant-Scalping-Strategy.md | 1 | The orders are strategy.order calls with a quantity of 1 in each direction (a 'buy' and a 'sel' order, each with its own tick bracket): strategy.order adds to and nets against the open position instead of reversing it, so a sell order while long only takes the position to flat and repeated signals stack units: net-position arithmetic that the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 436142 | 动态跟随止损策略Dynamic-Trailing-Stop-Loss-Strategy.md | 1 | The signals combine daily and 15-minute request.security reads inside while loops with break (high[_bar] / low[_bar] of several earlier bars, including the index 0 bar which on the 1-minute chart is the still-forming day), choosing which earlier range the previous close broke out of; which value each read returns on a historical bar depends on the runtime's handling of in-progress higher bars, and the stop sits at a structural 15-minute low. |
| 436217 | 双动量突破与波动率过滤量化策略Dual-Momentum-Breakthrough-and-Volatility-Filtering-Algorithmic-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | The entry filter reads atr(14) through security(tickerid, 'D', ...) and then a moving average of the percent ATR through a second security call whose expression is the variable atrp, itself built from the first security result and the chart close: a nested request.security whose evaluation context (daily versus chart bars) depends on how the runtime resolves the inner variable; the take profit (3000 %) and the 50 % trail are inert. |
| 436245 | 动态加仓策略Dynamic-Pyramiding-Strategy.md | 1 | A dollar-cost-averaging emulator: after the base order it adds safety orders (strategy.entry with growing cash quantities, volume scale 2, up to 10 levels, pyramiding 99) every time the price falls a step under the first entry, and closes everything at +1.5 % of the average price: a ladder of adds whose size depends on the position, which the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 436475 | 基于AO震荡指标的EMA交叉日内交易策略EMA-Crossover-Intraday-Trading-Strategy-Based-on-AO-Oscillator.md | 2 | Every trade is gated by a 03:00-11:00 session test (time(timeframe.period, session) != 0) on the DAILY chart of the backtest header, where a bar's own time is not inside the session, so the condition is a clock-time filter that is undefined (probably never true) on the chart it was tested on; close_all is also sent whenever the session is false. |
| 436482 | 超趋势增强旗形发现者An-Enhanced-Flag-Pattern-Recognition-Strategy-Integrating-with-SuperTrend.md | 1 | A flag-breakout finder whose only exit is strategy.exit with stop=supertrend: a stop level that moves every bar with the Supertrend line (re-sent each bar), a trailing level that is not a fraction of the fill price and cannot be expressed by the house stop interface; the flag and pole detection also depends on many persistent var indices tied to bar positions. |
| 436483 | BBMA突破策略BBMA-Breakthrough-Strategy.md | 2 | The long's stop sits above its own entry price and the limit target also sits above it, so with the fill at the next open the stop and the limit are both already crossed: trades open and close at the same open, the strategy has no defined result as a signal system. |
| 436515 | 50资金50仓位动态平衡量化策略Dynamic-Balancing-Strategy-with-50-Funds-and-50-Positions.md | 1 | A 50/50 dynamic balancing strategy that sends strategy.order calls to rebalance the position toward half the equity at each step: the size changes every bar depending on the position and equity, which the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 436524 | 基于RSI指标的多头趋势追踪策略RSI-Based-Bullish-Trend-Following-Strategy.md | 1 | An RSI-crossing strategy with pyramiding 5 whose exits are two strategy.exit orders for half of the position each (qty_percent 50 / 50, targets +3 % and +5 %, stop 5 %), and entries that stack up to five units; the position is built and unwound in parts, which the all-in / all-out signal interface cannot express. |
| 436528 | 双移动平均线黄金交叉策略Dual-Moving-Average-Crossover-Strategy.md | 1 | Orders are sent with strategy.order (one unit BUY after a bullish crossing above the 100-bar average, one unit SELL after a bearish crossing below it), not strategy.entry: each order adds to or subtracts from the net position without any direction logic, so a filtered-out crossing leaves the net at 0, +1, -1, -2 ...; the unit-by-unit accumulation cannot be expressed as flat / long / short signals. |
| 436603 | 基于趋势跟踪突破策略Trend-Tracking-Breakout-Strategy.md | 2 | The stop and the target are written as strategy.exit(loss = low - 5.70, profit = close + 11.40): price levels of one instrument passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are (price - 5.7) x tick size, an instrument-specific and nearly meaningless bracket (about 10 % at a 0.1 tick on a 40000 price, about 1 % at 0.01); the author's intended USD levels are tied to a single price scale, so there is no generic reading. |
| 436653 | 高杠杆防止追加保证金头寸管理策略Leveraged-Position-Sizing-with-Margin-Call-Risk-Management-Strategy.md | 1 | A leverage and margin-call management script: the position size is a leverage multiple of one unit, entries are blocked and strategy.close_all is sent when strategy.equity falls to a margin value (25000), and the trigger reads the high / low of the 4-hour bar through a lower-timeframe security call from a daily chart; the margin rule depends on the account equity, which the signal interface cannot see. |
| 436659 | 结合MACDRSI和FIB的量化比特币交易策略Quant-Bitcoin-Trading-Strategy-Combining-MACD-RSI-and-FIB.md | 2 | Every signal is computed on security(sym, '', close / high / low) where sym is an input whose default is BINANCE:BTCUSDT: a named external instrument used as the signal source for whatever chart is traded (SURVEY_README: another symbol -> criterion 2), with the entries sized from strategy.equity. |
| 436773 | 移动平均线交叉金策略Moving-Average-Crossover-Gold-Strategy.md | 2 | The stop, the target and the break-even exit are written as strategy.exit(loss = close x 0.99, profit = close x 1.02) and exit(loss = close x 1.01): price levels passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are (price x 0.99) x tick size, an instrument-specific and nearly meaningless bracket (about 10 % at a 0.1 tick on a 42000 price, about 1 % at 0.01); these are the only exits, so there is no generic reading (as #436603). |
| 436776 | 基于PSARMACD和RSI的多时间框架量化交易策略Multi-timeframe-Quantitative-Trading-Strategy-Based-on-PSAR-MACD-and-RSI.md | 1 | An intraday square-off script run on the daily header chart: entries are sent only while barInSession('0915-1455') is true, and every daily bar opens at its session start (00:00 UTC on the backtest exchange, 17:00 New York on broker days), outside 09:15-14:55, so no order can ever be sent and the square-off closes nothing; the bracket is also computed from strategy.position_avg_price before the entry fills (na when flat). |
| 436859 | 多时间框架判断趋势策略Multi-Timeframe-Trend-Strategy.md | 1 | Reads the future: Pine v2 request.security(syminfo.tickerid, 'D' / '5D' / '15D' / '45D', open / close) without [1] behaves as lookahead_on, so on every 1-hour bar the candle colours use the final close of the current day, 5-day, 15-day and 45-day bars, not known until they end (SURVEY_README request.security rule, as #432988). |
| 437027 | 动量指标交叉反转趋势跟踪策略Trend-Reversal-Momentum-Indicators-Crossover-Tracking-Strategy.md | 1 | Each trade is closed in two halves: strategy.exit(qty_percent = 50) at TP 1 (6 %) and the rest at TP 2 (12 %), both with a 3 % stop, and once the high reaches TP 1 the second half's stop is cancelled and re-placed at the entry price; partial exits and a stop moved after a partial fill are outside the single-position signal interface (as #436524). |
| 437048 | 动态趋势突破策略Dynamic-Breakout-Trend-Following-Strategy.md | 2 | The only exits are strategy.exit(profit = 0.02 x close, loss = 0.01 x close): percents of the close in PRICE units passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are 2 % / 1 % of the price times the tick size (0.2 % / 0.1 % at a 0.1 tick on BTC, 2 % / 1 % at a 1.0 tick), an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #436603, #436773). |
| 437411 | 日K线突破策略Daily-Breakout-Strategy.md | 1 | The only stop is strategy.exit(loss = 0.001 x lastDayOpen x tradeSize) with tradeSize = strategy.equity / 3500: the stop distance in ticks grows with the account equity, so the exit depends on the account state, which the signal interface cannot see (as #436653's equity-dependent rule); the order size is also taken from the equity. |
| 437487 | 玻尔布带ATR尾随止损策略Bollinger-Bands-ATR-Trailing-Stop-Strategy.md | 1 | Entries require strategy.position_size == 0, and the only exits are an exit(limit, stop) whose stop ratchets every bar on max(low - ATR(5), previous stop) (a trail on lows the house trail cannot express) together with a limit set from that stop's distance: whether the strategy is flat, and so whether it may enter, depends on an exit the port cannot model; the candle and midline tests are also in forex pips (x 0.0001) on a BTC chart. |
| 437544 | 收盘买入次日开盘止盈策略Close-Buying-Next-Open-Profit-Taking-Strategy.md | 1 | An intraday buy-at-the-close script on the daily header chart: the buy window time(period, '1429-1500') and the sell window '0925-0935' never contain a daily bar's open (00:00 UTC on the backtest exchange, 17:00 New York on broker days), so no entry is ever sent (as #436776). |
| 437557 | 基于移动平均线和相对强弱指标的动量反转策略Momentum-Reversal-Strategy.md | 1 | The take profit is strategy.exit(profit = 8, qty_percent = 50), a half-position exit, and the remainder closes on an SMA cross only on the bar the take profit is placed (quantityRemainder resets every bar): partial exits are outside the single-position signal interface (as #436524). |
| 437677 | TrendSurfing-双均线交叉趋势策略TrendSurfing-Double-Moving-Average-Crossover-Trend-Strategy.md | 2 | Apart from the reversing entries, the exits are strategy.exit(loss = close - 1 % x ATR, profit = close + 1 % x ATR): price LEVELS passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are about the whole price times the tick size (about 10 % at a 0.1 tick on a 42000 price), an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #436603, #436773). |
| 437738 | 基于EMA和MACD的多时间框架趋势跟踪策略Trend-Following-Strategy-Based-on-EMA-and-MACD-across-Timeframes.md | 2 | The exits are strategy.exit(loss = close - 1 ATR, profit = close + 3 ATR): price LEVELS passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are about the whole price times the tick size, an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #436603, #437677). |
| 437787 | 渔夫转身EMA多重止损多重止盈策略Fisher-Turnaround-EMA-Multi-Take-Profit-and-Multi-Stop-Strategy.md | 2 | The only exits are strategy.exit(loss = close, profit = close + 2 x ATR / close + 3 x ATR): price LEVELS passed as TICK distances, so the real distances are the price itself times the tick size (an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading, as #437048), and the two exits close 50 % and 100 % of the position (partial exits, criterion 1, as #436524). The Blackflag FTS value is always 50 (up and down are the same sum), so its filter is constant. |
| 438016 | 超趋势追踪止盈策略Supertrend-Take-Profit-Strategy.md | 1 | The only exits are three take-profit limits closing 25 %, 50 % and 25 % of the position (strategy.exit qty_percent): partial exits, which the house engine cannot hold (one position, all-in or all-out; as #436524). |
| 438017 | 基于K线收盘价的买卖策略Buy-Sell-on-Candle-Close-Strategy.md | 1 | pastLow / pastHigh = request.security(tickerid, "D", low / high, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on) with the [1] applied on the 1-hour chart, not inside the call: from the second hour of a day the value is that same day's final low / high, known only at the day's end (look-ahead). |
| 438025 | 质数波带回测策略Prime-Number-Bands-Backtest-Strategy.md | 2 | The bands are the nearest prime numbers above the high and below the low (integer steps of the quote unit; on fractional prices the price itself): a property of the quote's numeric scale, not of the market (as #430985, #430847). |
| 438481 | 基于RSI-V形态的短线盈利策略-RSI-V-shaped-Pattern-Swing-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | Exits close half and three quarters of the position on RSI crossings (strategy.close with qty = position_size x 1/2, x 3/4): partial exits, which the house engine cannot hold (as #436524). |
| 438495 | 趋势追踪型EMA突破策略Trend-Tracking-EMA-Breakout-Strategy.md | 1 | monthclose / weekclose / dayclose = request.security(tickerid, 'M' / 'W' / 'D', close, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on): on a daily chart the month's and week's FINAL close is used from their first bar (look-ahead); the entry requires monthclose > monthopen and weekclose > weekopen. |
| 439275 | 基于供需区域与EMA滑动止损的交易策略Trading-Strategy-Based-on-Supply-and-Demand-Zones-with-EMA-and-Trailing-Stop.md | 2 | The only stop is strategy.exit(loss = trailStop) with trailStop = close - 2 x ATR(14): a price LEVEL passed as a TICK distance, so the real distance is the price itself times the tick size (about 10 % of the price at a 0.1 tick on BTC, the whole price at a 1.0 tick), an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #437048, #437787). The daily supply / demand zones only colour the background. |
| 439360 | 双止盈双止损移动止损量化策略Dual-Take-Profit-Dual-Stop-Loss-Trailing-Stop-Loss-Bitcoin-Quantitative-Strategy.md | 1 | Every trade is closed by two exits: exit1 (qty_percent = 1, at SL1 / TP1) closes 1 % of the position and exit2 the rest at SL2 / TP2 with a stop that moves after TP1: partial exits (criterion 1, as #436524). The pip distance is also an input times ten ticks. |
| 439361 | 基于Nadaraya-Watson信封线和ROC指标的趋势跟踪策略Trend-Following-Strategy-Based-on-Nadaraya-Watson-Envelopes-and-ROC-Indicator.md | 2 | Apart from the reversing entries, the exits are strategy.exit(loss = close - 4 x 0.0001, profit = close + 8.4 x 0.0001): price LEVELS passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are about the whole price times the tick size, an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #437677, #437048). The entry tests close + 9 > 0 and close - 9 < 0 are price-unit thresholds (shorts only below a price of 9). |
| 439363 | 基于动能量化追趋策略Heiken-Ashi-Momentum-Quant-Strategy.md | 1 | Every signal reads request.security("M" / "1D", [...], lookahead_on) on a daily chart: the monthly Heikin-Ashi values come from the month still in progress (its final close), so the rules use the future (criterion 1, as #438495). |
| 439364 | 基于均线交叉的高频量化交易策略Intraday-Moving-Average-Crossover-Trading-Strategy.md | 2 | Apart from the reversing entries, the exits are strategy.exit(loss = close x 1 / 100, profit = close x 1 / 100): percents of the close in PRICE units passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are 1 % of the price times the tick size (0.1 % at a 0.1 tick on BTC, 1 % at a 1.0 tick), an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #437048, #437677). |
| 439366 | 比特币和黄金5分钟震荡交易策略20Bitcoin-and-Gold-5-Minute-Scalping-Strategy-20.md | 2 | The only exit is strategy.exit(loss = stopLossLevel) with stopLossLevel = lowest(low, 2)[1] - ATR(14): a price LEVEL passed as a TICK distance, so the real distance is about the whole price times the tick size, an instrument-specific stop with no generic reading (as #437787, #439275); strategy.close("Sell") names an id that is never entered. |

| 439610 | 影响周线的交易策略Weekly-Swing-Trading-Strategy.md | 2 | The take-profit exit trails with trail_price = 0.03 (a price LEVEL, met at once) and trail_offset = 0.03 TICKS: the intended 3 % trail is passed as a tick count, so the real trail is 0.03 x the tick size behind the best price, an instrument-specific trail with no generic reading (as #437048); the 5 % stop and 10 % target are re-sent every bar from the current close. |

| 439752 | 基于均线的震荡突破策略Oscillation-Breakthrough-Strategy-Based-on-Moving-Average.md | 1 | On the 1-day header chart every entry needs istradingsession = time('1', '0800-1600') to be defined, and a daily bar opens outside that session (its time is the session start), so the script as written never enters; an intraday reading is not the declared chart (as #430384). |
| 439753 | 震荡盈利波段Grid策略Profit-Grid-Strategy-With-Oscillation.md | 1 | As written (Long, 'Grid - reentry', every filter and exit '---'), the grid level starts at 0, so the second bar of the data already moves it up and enters; no exit is ever sent and pyramiding blocks further entries: a buy-and-hold from the start of whatever data is loaded, with no repeatable rule at the source's settings (as #430384). The 500 base-currency grid gap is also an instrument-specific price distance. |
| 439844 | 基于移动平均线金叉死叉交易策略Moving-Average-Crossover-Trading-Strategy.md | 2 | Apart from the reversing entries, the exits are strategy.exit(profit = close x 0.15, loss = close x 0.20): percents of the close in PRICE units passed as TICK counts, so the real distances are 15 % / 20 % of the price times the tick size (1.5 % / 2 % at a 0.1 tick on BTC, 15 % / 20 % at a 1.0 tick), an instrument-specific bracket with no generic reading (as #437048, #437677). |

| 439950 | 动量均线盘整策略Momentum-Moving-Average-Consolidation-Strategy.md | 1 | pyramiding 2 with an 'Add' entry below the average price and strategy.close(qty_percent = 20) partial profit-taking when the RSI crosses under 80: the position path and the exits are partial (criterion 1, as #436524). |

| 442822 | 基于订单块的动量突破策略The-Order-Block-Momentum-Breakout-Strategy.md | 1 | The only exits close when strategy.openprofit divided by the equity (initial capital plus netprofit) reaches +2 % or -1 %: an account-state gate whose price move depends on the position size against the equity, which the signal-only port cannot express (criterion 1, as #440336). |
| 442833 | RSI指标多空分离交易策略RSI-Indicator-Long-Short-Separation-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | pyramiding = 4 with a fixed quantity of 2 and repeated RSI-divergence entries while a position is open: the position accumulates units (criterion 1, as #442112, #430762). |
| 442836 | 追踪熊市策略Follow-The-Bear-Strategy.md | 1 | The pinbar test is replaced by star = true, so on every confirmed bar while flat the script sends a new short (its filters are computed but never used) and re-sets the bracket from that bar: a constant-true entry, a short re-entered whenever flat (criterion 1, as #442261). |
| 442860 | 动量追踪型双EMA移动平均线交叉策略Momentum-Tracking-Dual-EMA-Crossover-Strategy.md | 1 | Exits close parts of the position (qty_percent = 50 on the short) and every profit and stop exit is gated on strategy.openprofit over a cash order value of 1000: partial exits and cash-ratio exits (criterion 1, as #436524). |
| 442926 | 卖涨回落策略Sell-the-Rallies-Strategy.md | 1 | pyramiding = 2 with a short entry on every new 2 % one-bar rally while a short is open: the position accumulates units (criterion 1, as #441954, #430762). |
| 442940 | 支撑阻力趋势追踪策略Support-and-Resistance-Trend-Tracking-Strategy.md | 2 | The exits strategy.exit(loss = stopLossLevel, profit = targetPrice) pass price levels where the call takes tick distances, and the entries test the close against the fixed prices 100 / 200 (always above 200 on the BTC reading): undefined exits and instrument-specific levels (criterion 2, as #442076, #437048). |
| 443044 | 升降通道突破策略SuperTrend-Breakout-Strategy.md | 1 | is_uptrend (close > hlc3 - 3 ATR) and is_downtrend (close < hlc3 + 3 ATR) both hold on nearly every bar, so trend_changed is nearly always true and both entries fire together; the later Short wins (as #437773): a sell-and-hold from near the data start (criterion 1, as #442261). |
| 443110 | 多时间框架随机指数均线策略Multi-Timeframe-Stochastic-Strategy.md | 1 | pyramiding = 3 with repeated "Lungo" / "Corto" entries on each new stochastic crossing while a position is open (the trail_points-only exits place no trail): the position accumulates units (criterion 1, as #442112, #430762). |
| 443238 | 快速RSI反转交易策略Fast-RSI-Reversal-Trading-Strategy.md | 1 | pyramiding = 5 with the long entry allowed again whenever the close is under the average price (and the short whenever above it): averaging into a losing position, the position accumulates units (criterion 1, as #430762). |
| 443252 | 基于布林带的短线交易策略Short-Term-Trading-Strategy-Based-on-Bollinger-Bands.md | 2 | The exits strategy.exit(profit = close x (1 + 0.5 %), loss = close x (1 - 3 %)) pass price levels where the call takes tick distances, with no /mintick: the target and stop are undefined as written (criterion 2, as #442115, #437048). |
| 443990 | SSL通道与绿量策略SSL-Channel-and-Green-Volume-Strategy.md | 2 | The exits strategy.exit(loss = close x (1 - 0.5 %), profit = close x (1 + 1 %)) pass price levels where the call takes tick distances, with no /mintick: the stops and targets are undefined as written (criterion 2, as #442076, #437048). |
| 444024 | 基于随机RSI和EMA交叉的加密货币回调交易策略Crypto-Pullback-Trading-Strategy-Based-on-Stochastic-RSI-and-EMA-Crossover.md | 1 | pyramiding = 10 with level entries (close between the EMAs with the stochastic RSI under 25 / over 85) re-sent on every bar the condition holds: the position accumulates units (criterion 1, as #442112, #430762). |
| 444028 | 抛物线SAR趋势跟踪策略-60Parabolic-SAR-Trend-Tracking-Strategy-60.md | 1 | pyramiding = 5 with a new long on each SAR crossover while a long is open (the short needs a 2 % down bar): the position accumulates units (criterion 1, as #442112, #430762). |
| 444355 | 基于平均价海金阿什烛台与相对强弱指数的动态调仓策略Dynamic-Capital-Allocation-Strategy-Based-on-Heikin-Ashi-Candles-and-Relative-Strength-Index.md | 1 | pyramiding = 10000 with a 10 % of equity buy on every green Heikin Ashi candle after a red one until the RSI exit: dollar-cost averaging that accumulates units (criterion 1, as #442229, #430762). |
